*Ivan Viakhirev,Kirill Borodin,Amirah Almutairi,Serguei Barannikov,Maxim Abramov,Grach Mkrtchian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究循环深度推理模型（recurrent-depth reasoners）在测试时增加迭代次数对答案的影响，发现训练算子的有限时间动力学状态（settling/marginal/drifting）可以预测其深度安全性，并提出一个关于深度安全的充分条件以及四个用于判断测试时深度是否有用的操作标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环深度推理模型希望通过在测试时迭代更长时间来解决更难的问题，但实践中额外迭代可能改善、保持不变或降低答案质量。目前缺乏可预测的指标来判断何时增加深度是有益的，这使得深度外推行为不可控。

Method: 提出可测量的算子性质——有限时间动力学状态（settling/marginal/drifting）作为行为指示器；给出深度安全的充分条件（算子的每步位移相对于解码器边界足够小）；通过单一终端不动点目标来调控动力学状态与深度行为；提出四个操作标准用于分类失败模式，并应用于Huginn-3.5B作为一致性检验。

Result: 在每难度800个未增强样本的算法任务上，settling算子不会因增加深度而退化，在某些任务上还能将稀有难实例的准确率提升（如数独从0.19提升到0.34，超越训练范围）。移除终端不动点目标会导致漂移并消除增益，而加入它能使通用循环在进位传播任务上实现深度安全的外推。Huginn-3.5B属于非settling家族。

Conclusion: 算子的有限时间动力学状态是决定测试时深度行为的关键可测量属性，通过调节终端不动点目标可以实现深度安全的外推，并可据此诊断不同模型的深度利用潜力。

Abstract: Recurrent-depth reasoners aim to solve harder problems by iterating their update longer at test time, but additional iterations can improve, preserve, or degrade an answer. We show that a measurable property of the trained operator, its finite-time dynamical regime (estimated as settling, marginal, or drifting), indicates which of these occurs. We give a sufficient condition for depth-safety: once an operator's per-step displacement is small relative to the decoder margin, the decoded answer cannot change under further iterations. Empirically, on algorithmic tasks trained from $800$ unaugmented examples per difficulty tier, settling operators do not degrade with added depth, and on some tasks convert it into higher accuracy on harder unseen instances (Sudoku, $0.19$ to $0.34$ past the training horizon). A single terminal fixed-point objective moves the regime and the depth behavior together: removing it induces drift and removes the gains, and adding it to a generic recurrence yields depth-safe extrapolation on carry propagation. We give four operational criteria for useful test-time depth, use them to catalogue failure modes, and, as a consistency check, apply the same measurements to Huginn-3.5B, which falls in the non-settling family.

</details>


### [10] [Allocating Recurrent Compute in Looped Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18230)
*Ruhai Lin,Yiyang Guo,Rui-Jie Zhu,Hao Ye,Jason K. Eshraghian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MixerLoop架构，只需重复Gated DeltaNet混合器而无需重复稠密前馈网络（FFN），即可在减少45.9%循环骨干投影FLOPs的同时保留大部分循环深度的收益，在15M和110M参数规模下超越了全块循环（FullLoop）基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有循环语言模型通常重复整个层栈（包括混合器和FFN），但两者执行不同操作且成本不同。作者质疑：循环应该重复什么？他们通过迭代传输秩（ITR）和边际ITR来分析循环的价值，认为只有当应用能暴露新的跨位置影响方向且该方向在任务读出时仍可观测时，循环才有价值。

Method: 作者提出迭代传输秩（ITR）来描述累积影响轨迹，边际ITR描述连续应用带来的非冗余影响。基于此提出MixerLoop架构：重复每个Gated DeltaNet混合器，同时仅应用一次稠密FFN。在相同数据、初始化和架构条件下，对比无循环、全块循环和MixerLoop在15M和110M参数规模下的表现。

Result: MixerLoop在15M参数下在聚合CORE指标上超越FullLoop，在110M参数下保留了FullLoop CORE提升的41.5%，同时将循环骨干投影FLOPs减少了45.9%。有限上下文偏移干预实验表明，后续混合器应用能产生显著、非负且有益的最终语言模型读出变化。

Conclusion: 循环深度的收益可以在不重复执行稠密FFN的情况下保留，因为循环的价值主要来自混合器带来的新跨位置影响方向，而非FFN的重复应用。

Abstract: Looped language models improve reasoning and knowledge manipulation by applying shared computation repeatedly. Existing systems usually repeat an entire layer stack, although a mixer and a dense feed-forward network (FFN) perform different operations and have different costs. We ask a narrower question: what should loop? We view recurrence as repeated composition of a state update and argue that an application is valuable when it exposes a new cross-position influence direction that remains observable at the task readout. Iterative Transport Rank (ITR) describes the cumulative influence trajectory; marginal ITR describes the nonredundant influence contributed by successive applications. This view motivates MixerLoop, which repeats each Gated DeltaNet mixer while applying its dense FFN once. We compare MixerLoop with no recurrence and full-block recurrence at 15M and 110M parameters under the same data, initialization, and architecture. A finite context-off intervention tests whether later mixer applications produce distinct, non-negligible, and beneficial changes at the final language-model readout. MixerLoop surpasses FullLoop on aggregate CORE at 15M and retains 41.5% of its CORE improvement at 110M while reducing recurrent-backbone projection FLOPs by 45.9%. These results show that the benefits of recurrent depth can be retained without repeatedly executing the dense FFN.

</details>


### [11] [Classifying Directional Trajectories Near Criticality in the Three-State Majority-Vote Model with Deep Belief Networks and Bidirectional GRUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18235)
*Mauricio A. Valle,Gonzalo A. Ruz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了深度信念网络（DBN）和双向门控循环单元（Bi-GRU）在三态多数票模型（MV3）中对四种动态轨迹类型（从无序接近、从有序接近、向无序离开、向有序离开）的判别能力。DBN将格点快照编码为81维潜向量，Bi-GRU在DBN编码的时间序列上实现近完美的四类分离，并能实时感知系统的动态状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目的是探究无监督静态特征提取（DBN）与序列建模（Bi-GRU）相结合的分层架构能否有效地对MV3模型中不同的动力学轨迹类型进行分类，从而为智能体观点动力学模型中的临界转变检测建立原理性框架。

Method: 架构为高斯-伯努利受限玻尔兹曼机输入层 + DBN（784→4096→225→81）进行无监督预训练，将每个格点快照编码为81维潜向量；随后使用两层Bi-GRU分类器在长度为T=50的DBN编码快照序列上进行训练；采用t-SNE进行潜空间/隐状态空间可视化，并通过滑动窗口方式将训练好的Bi-GRU应用于连续的MV3动力学过程以实现实时传感。

Result: t-SNE分析显示DBN潜空间对四种轨迹类型仅有部分分离，表明仅基于静态配置训练的模型无法完全解析方向性时间结构；而Bi-GRU分类器在训练集和测试集上均实现了四种轨迹类型的近完美分离，滑动窗口应用也证明其能实时感知系统当前动力学状态。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了用于智能体观点动力学模型中临界转变检测与分类的分层架构（静态特征提取+顺序建模），验证了该架构的有效性和实时检测能力。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate whether the latent representations learned by a Deep Belief Network (DBN) and a Bidirectional Gated Recurrent Unit (Bi-GRU) can discriminate among four dynamically distinct trajectory types in the three-state majority vote model (MV3): approach from disorder, approach from order, departure to disorder, and departure to order. The DBN, pre-trained in an unsupervised manner on static equilibrium samples via a Gaussian-Bernoulli Restricted Boltzmann Machine input layer and architecture $784 \to 4096 \to 225 \to 81$, encodes each lattice snapshot into an 81-dimensional latent vector. A t-SNE analysis of the DBN latent space reveals only partial separation of the four trajectory types, reflecting the fact that a model trained on static configurations cannot fully resolve directional temporal structure. A two-layer Bi-GRU classifier, trained on sequences of DBN-encoded snapshots of length $T = 50$, achieves near-perfect separation of all four trajectory types in its hidden state space, as confirmed by t-SNE visualization on both training and test sets. Furthermore, a sliding-window application of the trained Bi-GRU to continuous MV3 dynamics demonstrates its ability to sense the system's current dynamical regime in real-time. These results establish a principled hierarchical architecture for detecting and classifying critical transitions in agent-based opinion dynamics models.

</details>


### [12] [ClosureBench: A Constructive Benchmark for Compositional Graph Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18242)
*Stefano Goria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ClosureBench，一个基于程序化验证的构造式组合图关系推理基准，通过按需生成实例避免数据污染，实验发现模型存在记忆化缺口、图-查询深度交互瓶颈，以及程序合成训练可显著提升推理鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有组合图关系推理基准依赖固定测试集，易受数据污染（如预训练泄露或微调记忆）影响，无法可靠评估模型的真实推理能力；同时缺乏能够按需生成、机器验证答案的基准来直接衡量记忆化程度与推理瓶颈。

Method: 构建ClosureBench，使用Ein张量逻辑语言程序计算参考答案以实现机器级正确性验证；按需生成实例跨越26个任务类别、3个组合层级（L1-L3），沿图规模、边密度和查询深度三个独立轴控制难度；评估从1.5B开源到前沿系统模型，并对比固定测试集微调与程序合成微调（Ein和Python+NetworkX两种目标）的效果。

Result: 三项主要发现：（1）在固定测试集上微调的模型在已见与新鲜实例上准确率相差19.3个百分点，直接证明记忆化现象存在于监督微调中；（2）准确率随图规模和查询深度增加而下降，二者交互作用显著，模型先从自然语言描述中误读图结构再基于错误图进行正确推理，即使最前沿模型也从原子查询退化到组合查询，且该瓶颈与输入格式（文本、JSON边列表、邻接矩阵）无关；（3）4B模型微调为输出可执行程序后，跨组合层级准确率几乎持平，在保留实例上达到94.3%，接近前沿模型水平且token成本大幅降低，对Ein和Python+NetworkX两种程序目标均成立。

Conclusion: ClosureBench通过程序化验证与按需生成机制为图关系推理提供了抗污染、可重组的评估框架；实验揭示了大模型在图解析环节的核心薄弱点，并证明经过验证的程序合成训练是一种比答案监督更具鲁棒性且高效的推理能力提升路径。

Abstract: We introduce ClosureBench, a constructive benchmark for compositional graph-relational reasoning with programmatically verified ground truth. Unlike fixed-test-set benchmarks vulnerable to data contamination, ClosureBench generates instances on demand: each task's reference answer is computed by executing a program in the Ein tensor-logic language, ensuring machine-verified correctness. The benchmark spans 26 task categories at three compositional levels (L1-L3), with difficulty controlled along three independent axes: graph size, edge density, and query depth.
  We evaluate models from 1.5B open weights to frontier systems (o3, GPT-4.1, Gemini 2.5, Claude Sonnet 4) and report three findings. First, because the benchmark can always supply fresh instances, it measures memorisation directly: a model fine-tuned on a fixed test set shows a 19.3 percentage-point gap between its accuracy on seen and on fresh instances, which a static test set cannot reveal. We scope this to supervised fine-tuning on answer pairs, not pretraining contamination. Second, accuracy falls as graph size and query depth increase, and the two interact: models misread the graph from its natural-language description and then reason correctly over the wrong graph, so even the strongest frontier model degrades from atomic to compositional queries. This bottleneck is a property of the reasoning rather than the input format: it persists when the graph is given as a JSON edge list or an adjacency matrix instead of prose. Third, a 4B model fine-tuned to emit executable programs rather than answers stays nearly flat across compositional levels and approaches frontier accuracy (94.3% on held-out instances) at a fraction of the token cost. This holds for two program targets, Ein and Python+NetworkX, so it is a property of verified program synthesis rather than of one language.

</details>


### [13] [Bidirectional representational alignment between biological and artificial neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18244)
*Samuel Kostousov,Abhinn Kaushik,Brokoslaw Laschowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过光谱正则化方法调控表征几何，显著提高了生物神经网络与人工神经网络之间的双向表征对齐性，实现了55%的相对改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明生物与人工神经网络之间的表征对齐是不对称的：模型表征预测神经反应的能力远强于神经反应预测模型表征的能力。这种不对称性引出一个核心问题：表征几何结构是否影响双向对齐。研究者假设在训练过程中引导表征几何可以系统地影响双向对齐。

Method: 开发了一个整合光谱正则化与双向预测性分析的通用计算框架，并在自监督对比视觉模型上进行验证。通过引导所学表征的光谱几何结构（如有效维度、共享表征子空间重组）来调控表征的对齐特性。

Result: 调控表征的光谱几何显著提升了反向预测性，同时正向预测性仅有适度下降，使双向预测性相对提升55%。这些改进伴随有效维度降低和共享表征子空间的重组，在中等光谱指数下正向与反向预测性趋于对称。

Conclusion: 表征几何可以被系统地调控以调节生物与人工神经网络之间的双向表征对齐，为理解和优化跨物种神经表征映射提供了新的途径。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that representational alignment between biological and artificial neural networks is asymmetric: model representations predict neural responses much better than neural responses predict model representations. This asymmetry raises the question of whether representational geometry contributes to bidirectional representational alignment. We hypothesized that steering representational geometry during training can systematically influence bidirectional alignment. To test this hypothesis, we developed a computational framework that integrates spectral regularization with bidirectional predictivity analyses. As an initial demonstration, we evaluated our framework using self-supervised contrastive vision models. Steering the spectral geometry of the learned representations substantially increased reverse predictivity with modest reductions in forward predictivity, yielding a 55% relative improvement in bidirectional predictivity. These improvements were accompanied by reduced effective dimensionality and a reorganization of the shared representational subspace, within which forward and reverse predictivity became approximately symmetric at intermediate spectral exponents. Overall, these findings demonstrate that representational geometry can be systematically steered to modulate bidirectional representational alignment between biological and artificial neural networks.

</details>


### [14] [FedLNS: Leverage LayerNorm Signature Modeling to Mitigate Adversarial Manipulation in Federated LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18736)
*Kai Li,Jong-Ik Park,Carlee Joe-Wong,Wei Ni,Falko Dressler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedLNS是一种服务器端的轻量级恶意更新筛查框架，通过监控可训练归一化层参数的签名变化来识别联邦学习中的恶意客户端更新，无需额外通信开销、原始数据或预训练检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦训练中服务器无法直接验证客户端本地监督或优化过程，恶意客户端可操纵训练目标、引入错误的上下文-词元关联，并通过重复聚合降低全局模型性能，增加幻觉生成风险。现有防护方法需要额外开销或依赖特定假设。

Method: FedLNS通过可训练归一化层参数的变化来表示每个客户端更新，并利用鲁棒的、历史感知的跨客户端参考来筛查可疑更新。签名在服务器端从返回的局部模型中提取，不增加额外通信开销；筛查后的完整模型更新可通过标准联邦聚合或其他兼容规则进行聚合。

Result: 在GPT风格、BERT风格和LLaMA风格模型（200个客户端从零训练）上的实验表明，在40%人口级目标操纵下，FedLNS在IID和非IID数据分区下对三种架构均比六个基线中最强者实现了更低的测试困惑度。

Conclusion: FedLNS是一种无需原始客户端数据、可信服务器数据集、标记攻击样本或单独训练检测器的有效恶意更新筛查方法，能够在保持标准联邦学习通信开销的同时防御恶意客户端攻击。

Abstract: Federated training enables language models to learn from distributed private text, but the server cannot directly verify the local supervision or optimization process that produces each client update. A malicious client can therefore train on corrupted targets, introduce incorrect context-token associations, and degrade the global model through repeated aggregation. Such degradation can also increase the risk of unreliable or hallucinatory generation. We propose Federated Learning with Normalization Signatures (FedLNS), a server-side framework for lightweight malicious-update screening. FedLNS represents each client update through changes in trainable normalization-layer parameters and screens suspicious updates against a robust, history-aware cross-client reference. Because the signatures are extracted at the server from the returned local models, FedLNS requires no additional client-to-server parameter or metadata exchange compared to standard federated learning (FL) methods. After screening, the retained full-model updates can be aggregated using standard FL or another compatible aggregation rule. FedLNS requires no raw client data, trusted server dataset, labeled attack examples, or separately trained detector. Experiments on GPT-style, BERT-style, and LLaMA-style models trained from scratch with 200 clients show that, under 40% population-level target manipulation, FedLNS achieves lower test perplexity than the strongest of six baselines for all three architectures under both IID (independently and identically distributed) and non-IID data partitions.

</details>


### [15] [SIGMA: Symmetry-aware, Intelligent, Geometric, Multi-objective Adaptive Control for Robust, Dependable Traffic Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18263)
*Pratham Payra,Jagadish B,Tanmay Sen,Tanujit Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SIGMA，一个结合大语言模型（LLM）的强化学习框架，用于自适应多目标交通信号控制。SIGMA将自然语言紧急指令转换为优先级向量，通过旋转增强实现跨路口泛化，并采用离线到在线学习确保稳定初始化与渐进适应，在SUMO模拟中于四个Kolkata城市路口上优于固定配时、感应控制和DQN基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通信号控制是复杂的序贯决策问题，需要在通行能力、延误公平性、信号稳定性和应急车辆优先权之间实时权衡。现有强化学习方法通常固定优化目标、忽略动态优先级变化，且难以在几何相似的路口间泛化，缺乏对目标自适应和系统鲁棒性的考虑。

Method: SIGMA框架包含三个关键组件：(1) 利用LLM将自然语言紧急指令解析为优先级向量，驱动多目标actor-critic控制器，避免人工奖励工程；(2) 旋转数据增强（rotational augmentation）提升方法在四向路口间的迁移能力；(3) 离线到在线学习（offline-to-online），先以稳定初始化再渐进适应动态交通变化。作者还定义了涵盖应急服务水平、LLM故障下降级和需求敏感性的可靠性属性，并通过bootstrap统计进行验证。

Result: 在SUMO中对四个Kolkata城市路口进行仿真评估，SIGMA相比固定配时、感应控制和DQN控制器，在平均/应急等待时间、排队长度方面均有降低，并提升了吞吐量。消融实验证实了其对组件故障和几何旋转的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SIGMA提供了一种可靠、语言引导的多目标交通信号控制系统，具备统计可靠性保证，在动态需求和应急优先场景下展现出显著优势，为语言模型与交通控制强化学习的结合提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Traffic signal control is a complex sequential decision-making problem requiring real-time adaptation and trade-offs among throughput, delay fairness, signal stability, and emergency vehicle priority. Existing RL methods often fix objectives, ignore dynamic priority changes, and fail to generalize across geometrically similar intersections.We propose SIGMA (Symmetry-aware, Intelligent, Geometric, Multi-objective Adaptive traffic control), an RL framework enhanced with a large language model (LLM) for adaptive objective tuning and orientation-invariant learning. SIGMA converts natural-language emergency commands into priority vectors for a multi-objective actor-critic controller, avoiding manual reward engineering. Rotational augmentation improves transferability across four-way intersections, while offline-to-online learning ensures stable initialization and gradual adaptation to changing traffic.We define reliability properties covering emergency service levels, graceful degradation under LLM failures, and demand sensitivity, validated via bootstrap statistics. Evaluated in SUMO on four Kolkata-based urban intersections against fixed-time, actuated, and DQN controllers, SIGMA reduces average/emergency waiting times and queue lengths, and boosts throughput. Ablation studies confirm robustness to component failures and geometric rotations. Overall, SIGMA offers a reliable, language-guided, multi-objective traffic control system with statistical reliability assurance.

</details>


### [16] [Rethinking Privileged Information in On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18271)
*Samyak Shrestha,Alexander Tessier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在线策略自蒸馏（OPSD）中，学生模型能否真正学习到特权参考信息，还是仅仅恢复了基座模型已有的推理行为。实验表明，正确的参考信息并不能一致地带来性能提升，学生的预测与基座模型思维行为的对齐程度甚至强于与参考监督的对齐程度，性能和分布对齐均无法单独判定参考信息对学习的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD通过让学生在自己的响应上训练，并使用同一模型基于特权参考信息提供的token级监督来提升性能。作者质疑：这些性能提升是否真的表明学生学到了参考中的信息，还是仅仅反映了基座模型中已存在的推理行为被恢复？这一问题关系到OPSD方法有效性的根本解释，需要严谨的分析框架来区分参考引入的监督与教师本身（无参考）提供的监督。

Method: 研究者使用Qwen3模型（1.7B到8B）在科学和数学数据集上进行OPSD实验。分析框架将参考信息引起的监督与无参考情况下教师提供的监督分离开，分别衡量它们与学生预测变化的对齐程度。此外还设置了对照实验，使用其他问题的参考信息和控制条件来验证参考特有贡献。

Result: 实验发现：正确参考信息在教师生成模式、模型规模和训练数据集之间并未提供一致的性能增益；学生在没有正确参考的情况下也能提升；在多个数学推理基准上，其他问题的解甚至优于正确解。学生预测与基座模型思维行为的对齐强于与参考引起的监督的对齐，但其他问题构造的对照组也能复现大部分对齐。归因于正确参考的更强对齐并不稳定地对应于更大的性能收益。

Conclusion: 仅凭性能增益和分布对齐无法判定特权参考信息在OPSD中对学生学习的贡献机制，需要对OPSD的内部学习机制进行更深入的分析和解释。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) trains a student on its own responses using token-level supervision from the same model conditioned on privileged reference information. We investigate whether performance gains from OPSD show that the student learned the information in the reference or instead reflect recovery of reasoning behavior already present in the base model. We perform OPSD experiments on science and mathematics datasets using Qwen3 models ranging from 1.7B to 8B. Our analysis framework separates the supervision induced by the reference from the supervision provided by the teacher without the reference and measures how each aligns with changes in the student's predictions. The correct reference does not provide a consistent performance benefit across teacher generation modes, model sizes, and training datasets. Students can improve without the correct reference, and a solution from another problem can outperform the correct solution on several mathematical reasoning benchmarks. The student's predictions align more strongly with the base model's thinking behavior than with the supervision induced by the reference, but controls constructed from other problems reproduce much of both alignments. Moreover, stronger alignment attributable to the correct reference does not reliably coincide with a greater performance benefit from the reference. Performance gains and distributional alignment alone therefore cannot determine how privileged reference information contributes to student learning in OPSD.

</details>


### [17] [SingularClip: Preventing Spectral Collapse to Maintain Plasticity in Continual and Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18319)
*Tyler Kastner,Nimrod De La Vega,Amir-massoud Farahmand*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现神经网络在非平稳任务训练中可塑性丧失的一个新来源——权重矩阵奇异值各向异性（anisotropy）随训练逐渐增大，并提出SingularClip方法，通过周期性裁剪所有权重矩阵的奇异值来缓解该问题，在持续监督学习和深度强化学习的多个任务中表现优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络在非平稳任务上训练时经常丧失适应新目标的能力（即可塑性丧失），这一问题严重限制了模型在持续学习和强化学习等场景中的表现。已有研究虽提出了多种解释和缓解方法，但对可塑性丧失的机制理解仍不完整，需要识别新的来源并开发有效的干预手段。

Method: 本文首先从经验和理论上分析权重矩阵奇异值各向异性增长这一可塑性丧失的新机制；随后提出SingularClip算法，在每个训练阶段周期性对网络中所有权重矩阵的奇异值进行裁剪（clipping），以限制各向异性增长并维持权重空间的表达能力。

Result: 在持续监督学习和深度强化学习的一系列任务中，SingularClip相比基线方法表现强劲，验证了奇异值各向异性作为可塑性丧失来源的有效性，以及奇异值裁剪作为缓解策略的可行性。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了权重矩阵奇异值各向异性是导致神经网络可塑性丧失的重要因素，并提出了一种简单有效的SingularClip方法，为持续学习和强化学习中的可塑性维持提供了新的理论视角和实用工具。

Abstract: Neural networks trained on nonstationary tasks frequently lose the ability to fit new targets, a phenomenon referred to as loss of plasticity. We identify a novel source of plasticity loss due to the growing anisotropy of weight matrices' singular values during training, and analyze this phenomenon both empirically and theoretically. To mitigate this issue, we introduce SingularClip, a procedure that periodically clips the singular values of all weight matrices. We show that SingularClip performs strongly against baselines across a range of tasks in both continual supervised learning and deep reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [18] [When Does Dynamic Ensembling Pay Off? Diagnosing Regionwise Gains in Regression under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18330)
*Tianxin Zhou,Ruixi Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 $\widehat{D}_{\mathrm{CF5}}$，利用小规模标记目标域探针数据来估计"按区域动态分配回归模型池信任"相对"静态最优混合"的可实现收益（区域级凸组合增益），预测精度在12个数据集-偏移对上达到Spearman +0.98，并由此构建"探针验证集成选择器"，在预注册前瞻批次中全部12次运行匹配或改善静态基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 输入依赖（动态）的回归模型组合是否优于最佳静态混合，取决于分布偏移的性质，且通常无法在部署前获知。作者希望回答：一个小的标记目标域探针数据，能否提前告诉我们"在输入空间的不同区域重新分配信任"是否值得？

Method: 提出 $\widehat{D}_{\mathrm{CF5}}$ 估计量，从探针数据交叉拟合估计区域级凸组合增益（即动态组合相对最优静态凸混合的可实现收益）；基于数据中手动提取的泛函近似（近似区域无关）。构建 Probe-Validated Ensemble Selector：在静态仿射堆叠器与动态实现器之间选择，仅当留出下置信界超过静态凸基线时才部署候选模型。发布 OpenRegShift 可复现评估框架，涵盖时空、领域、特征簇等12种（及16种敏感性）分布偏移设定，并引入受控生成器以验证动态增益机制。

Result: 在12个冻结的数据集-偏移对上，$\widehat{D}_{\mathrm{CF5}}$ 预测区域级测试增益的数据集级 Spearman 相关系数为 +0.98（95%CI [+0.83,+1.00], p=5×10^{-5}），其中两个案例推翻了预注册预期；16对敏感性分析中 Spearman +0.83，优于替代探针诊断的最高 +0.66。该对比隔离了"区域信任重分配"效应：区域凸组合增益相关 +0.98，而仿射校正后的平滑协变量依赖堆叠仅 +0.01。受控生成器表明动态增益源于偏移异质性与局部能力的交互，随偏移严重度增加，且需128到256个探针标签才可实现。前瞻批次中12次运行全部匹配或改善静态基线，两次部署分别降低测试风险11%和16%，而门控拒绝了一个若未门控部署会损失30倍以上静态损失的候选。

Conclusion: 小规模目标域探针确实可用于预估区域级动态模型组合的可实现收益，$\widehat{D}_{\mathrm{CF5}}$ 为部署前的"是否开启动态信任重分配"提供了可靠的决策依据；通过门控机制（Probe-Validated Ensemble Selector）可在保证不劣于静态基线的同时捕捉动态增益，OpenRegShift 框架为此类评估提供了标准化基准。

Abstract: Whether input-dependent ("dynamic") combination of a regression model pool beats the best static blend depends on the shift and is rarely known before deployment. Can a small labeled target-domain probe tell us when reallocating trust across regions of the input space will pay off? We answer this with $\widehat{D}_{\mathrm{CF5}}$, which estimates from the probe the cross-fitted gain of the regionwise convex combination over the best static convex blend: the realizable value of deciding, region by region, whom to trust. Across a frozen suite of 12 dataset-shift pairs (spatial, temporal, domain, feature-cluster), $\widehat{D}_{\mathrm{CF5}}$ predicts realized regionwise test gains with dataset-level Spearman $+0.98$ (95% CI $[+0.83, +1.00]$; $p=5\times10^{-5}$), including two cases overturning preregistered expectations. The relationship holds in a 16-pair sensitivity analysis (Spearman $+0.83$), whereas alternative probe diagnostics reach at most $+0.66$. This contrast isolates regional trust reallocation: correlation is $+0.98$ for regionwise-convex gain, but $+0.01$ for smooth covariate-dependent stacking after affine correction. A controlled generator shows dynamic gains arise from the interaction of shift heterogeneity and local competence, increase with shift severity, and become realizable between 128 and 256 probe labels in the tested grid. The Probe-Validated Ensemble Selector chooses among a static affine stacker and dynamic realizers, deploying a candidate only when a held-out lower confidence bound clears the static-convex floor. In a preregistered prospective batch, it matched or improved the floor in all 12 runs; two deployments reduced test risk by 11% and 16%, while the gate rejected a candidate whose un-gated deployment incurred $>30\times$ the static loss. We release OpenRegShift, a reproducible evaluation harness for regression ensembles under distribution shift.

</details>


### [19] [Selection, Recombination, or a Fresh Solve? A Candidate-Free Control for Single-Pass Test-Time Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18379)
*Guiv Farmanfarmaian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究在相同最大输出token预算下引入"无候选项"对照组，评估候选上下文对聚合模型的价值。在AIME-2025和HMMT-2025上使用Qwen3-4B发现：当多个正确候选项存在时，候选项条件化提升准确率（+0.290）；当所有候选项都错误时，候选项条件化反而降低准确率（-0.123）；单一正确候选项的情形结论不明确。研究还探究了显式答案字段对输出的因果引导作用，但掩蔽处理无显著收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当所有候选项都错误时，无法进行"正确候选选择"，但聚合过程仍可重新求解问题。一个正确的聚合答案可能来自重组（recombination）、重新求解或两者兼具。为高效测试时推理，关键问题是：在额外的生成过程之外，候选上下文是否增加了价值。现有研究缺乏在相同输出token预算下的无候选对照组约束设置。

Method: 引入缺失的"无候选"对照组，控制相同的最大输出token预算，并按正确候选数量分层分析。在AIME-2025和HMMT-2025两个数学基准上使用Qwen3-4B模型进行实验，并采用自适应双基准流程的保守校正。同时进行了原始格式匹配（original-format matching）和安慰剂实验（placebo tests）描述性表征失败模式，并在独立的结构化干预中检验显式答案字段的因果作用。

Result: 候选条件化在多个正确候选时显著提升准确率（Δ_cand(c2+)=+0.290），在全部候选错误时降低准确率（Δ_cand(c0)=-0.123），单一正确候选情形未决。c2+和c0结论通过保守校正后仍然成立。全错恢复（all-wrong recovery）的解释在该规模下发生逆转：条件化于全错候选池反而比重新求解准确率更低。显式答案字段因果性地引导输出，但掩蔽处理无可测量的准确率提升，与原始格式的等价性未得证。

Conclusion: 候选上下文的价值取决于候选池中正确候选的数量：多方正确时有用，全员错误时有害。所谓"全错恢复"现象在该模型规模下实质上是重新求解优于条件化生成。证据局限于单一模型族、两个数学基准、首答截断的候选片段和单轮提示式聚合，结论的外推需谨慎。

Abstract: When every candidate is wrong, correct-candidate selection is unavailable, yet the aggregation call can still solve the problem afresh. A correct aggregate answer may therefore reflect recombination, fresh solving, or both. For efficient test-time reasoning, the relevant question is whether candidate context adds value beyond the additional generation pass. We introduce the missing candidate-free control under the same maximum output-token allowance and stratify by the number of correct candidates. Across AIME-2025 and HMMT-2025 with Qwen3-4B, candidate conditioning improves accuracy when multiple candidates are correct ($Δ_{\mathrm{cand}}$(c2+) = +0.290), lowers accuracy when every candidate is wrong ($Δ_{\mathrm{cand}}$(c0) = -0.123), and remains unresolved in the one-correct regime. The c2+ and c0 conclusions survive a conservative correction for the adaptive two-benchmark procedure. Under this counterfactual, the interpretation of all-wrong recovery reverses at this scale: conditioning on an all-wrong candidate pool lowers accuracy relative to a fresh solve. Original-format matching and placebo results characterize the failures descriptively but leave their mechanism unresolved. Within a separate structured intervention, explicit answer fields causally steer outputs toward their values; masking yields no measurable accuracy improvement, and equivalence with the original format was not established. The evidence is limited to one Qwen3-4B family, two mathematics benchmarks, first-answer-truncated candidate fragments, and single-pass prompted aggregation.

</details>


### [20] [Vector Symbolic Policy Gradient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18404)
*Ryozo Masukawa,Sanggeon Yun,SungHeon Jeong,Hyunwoo Oh,Raheeb Hassan,Pietro Mercati,Nathaniel D. Bastian,Mahdi Imani,Mohsen Imani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出向量符号策略梯度（VSPG），一种离散动作actor，每个动作由单位范数超向量表示，通过相似度打分。在标准softmax策略梯度代理下，其更新等价于优势加权的超向量捆绑后归一化，并可作为固定大小的压缩核记忆存储优势加权核展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索向量符号架构（VSA）的动作记忆如何在策略梯度框架中提供样本高效的强化学习，同时保持推理时内存开销不变，并建立VSA行动记忆、对数线性策略梯度和核策略搜索之间的理论联系。

Method: VSPG将每个动作编码为单位范数超向量，用其与编码状态的相似度作为动作得分。论文证明了在标准softmax策略梯度代理下，其更新精确等价于优势加权超向量捆绑（bundling）后归一化，支持标准优势估计器。每个训练后的动作超向量是一个固定大小的压缩核记忆，存储优势加权核展开并依据编码器诱导的相似度传递证据。同时对双极动作记忆，证明在随机比特翻转下贪心动作选择的稳定性，失败概率随超向量维度指数衰减。

Result: VSPG的更新被严格推导为优势加权超向量捆绑加归一化，每个动作超向量被证明是一个固定大小的压缩核记忆（advantage-weighted kernel memory），能够在不增加推理内存的前提下支持样本高效学习。此外，对双极动作记忆给出了鲁棒性理论保证：随机比特翻转导致贪心选择失败的概率随超向量维度指数衰减。

Conclusion: VSPG统一了VSA动作记忆、对数线性策略梯度和核策略搜索三个方向，提供了定量的鲁棒性保证，为在连续状态空间中实现样本高效且内存友好的离散动作强化学习提供了新途径。

Abstract: We answer this question with Vector-Symbolic Policy Gradient (VSPG), a discrete-action actor that represents each action by a unit-norm hypervector and scores it by similarity to the encoded state. Under the standard softmax policy-gradient surrogate, we prove that its update is exactly advantage-weighted hypervector bundling followed by normalization, and therefore supports standard advantage estimators. We further show that each trained action hypervector is a fixed-size compressed kernel memory, storing an advantage-weighted kernel expansion over visited states and transferring evidence according to the encoder-induced similarity. This provides a concrete mechanism that can support sample-efficient learning without increasing inference-time memory. Finally, for bipolar action memories, we prove that greedy action selection is stable under random bit flips, with failure probability decaying exponentially in the hypervector dimension. VSPG thus connects VSA action memories, log-linear policy gradients, and kernel policy search while providing a quantitative robustness guarantee.

</details>


### [21] [Role-Conditioned Sub-Token Routing for Efficient Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18410)
*Wei Jiang,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RoleSub（Role-Conditioned Sub-Token Routing），一种针对Vision-Language-Action (VLA)模型的高效压缩方法。与现有方法主要减少视觉token数量不同，RoleSub在保留更多token的同时压缩其值表示的宽度，通过正交空间中按角色条件学习的路由机制决定哪些子token组被保留，可同时应用于视觉和语言表示。在OpenVLA-OFT-7B上四个LIBERO套件的36个设置中，33个设置优于仅做token裁剪的基线，在激进压缩下总KV可降到原始的9.2%-11.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型处理长多模态token序列，推理在内存和计算上开销巨大。现有效率方法主要依赖视觉token缩减，但激进的token剪枝会完全丢弃token的表示，导致性能脆弱。子token压缩作为补充方案保留更多token但降低值宽度，然而直接应用于VLA策略效果不佳，因为感知、语言理解与控制所需的信息在多模态表示中分布不均，需要根据角色进行差异化压缩。

Method: RoleSub的核心方法包括：(1) 在视觉token缩减后，将每个保留token的值表示在正交空间中划分为若干组；(2) 使用轻量路由器决定哪些组需要保留，路由决策由token表示、学习到的潜在角色表示和语言上下文共同条件化；(3) 同一机制也可应用于语言token的值表示，使视觉和语言表示均可被压缩而无需移除额外token，实现跨模态的统一ROI感知压缩。

Result: 在OpenVLA-OFT-7B上对四个LIBERO套件进行全面评估。在匹配视觉KV预算下，RoleSub在36个设置中33个优于训练过的纯token裁剪对照方法，且在激进压缩下收益最大。结合视觉和语言压缩后，总KV量降到原始的9.2%-11.3%，同时在大多数任务上保持强大的控制性能。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，在保留token内部缩减值表示的宽度，是token剪枝在激进VLA压缩场景下有效且互补的替代方案。RoleSub通过角色条件化的子token路由，实现了在不移除token的前提下对多模态表示的多粒度压缩，为VLA模型的高效推理提供了新思路。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models process long multimodal token sequences, making inference expensive in both memory and computation. Existing efficiency methods mainly reduce visual tokens, but aggressive token pruning becomes fragile because removing a token discards its entire representation. Sub-token compression provides a complementary alternative by retaining more tokens while reducing their value width. However, directly applying sub-token compression to VLA policies is less effective because information important for perception, language understanding, and control is distributed differently across the multimodal representation.
  We introduce Role-Conditioned Sub-Token Routing (RoleSub), which learns how to compress the value representations of retained tokens. After visual token reduction, RoleSub partitions each retained value representation into groups in an orthogonal space and uses a lightweight router to determine which groups should be preserved. The routing decision is conditioned on the token representation, a learned latent role representation, and language context. The same mechanism can also be applied to language values, allowing visual and language representations to be compressed without removing additional tokens.
  We evaluate RoleSub on OpenVLA-OFT-7B across the four LIBERO suites. At matched visual-KV budgets, RoleSub outperforms a trained token-only control in 33 of 36 settings, with the largest gains under aggressive compression. Combining visual and language compression reduces total KV to 9.2--11.3% of the original while retaining strong control performance on most tasks. These results show that reducing the representation within retained tokens provides an effective complement to token pruning for aggressive VLA compression.

</details>


### [22] [The Road Taken: The Role of Optimizers at the Edge of Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18415)
*Jaerin Lee,Kyoung Mu Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现梯度下降等一阶优化器在实际训练中显著违反经典边缘稳定性（EoS）理论所预测的稳定性边界（偏差因子可达21.1），且该偏差具有系统性并与优化器类型高度相关。作者提出从方向Hessian和梯度对齐分数（基于优化器实际更新的方向）重新定义"实现边缘稳定性"（realized edge of stability），消除了优化器依赖的系统偏移，并提供新诊断工具揭示优化器在时间与空间预算间的平衡作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的边缘稳定性理论以最大Hessian特征值和学习率的乘积作为稳定性阈值，但在实践中发现该理论预测严重偏离实际，且偏离程度随优化器不同而系统变化。现有理论框架无法解释这一现象，因此需要一种新的稳定性阈值表述来更准确地刻画实际训练行为。

Method: 从方向Hessian（directional Hessian）出发，结合梯度对齐分数（gradient-alignment score），以优化器实际采取的更新方向（而非最大曲率模态）为基准，推导新的稳定性阈值公式，提出"实现边缘稳定性"（realized edge of stability）概念。

Result: 实验表明梯度下降等一阶方法对经典稳定性界限的违反程度可达21.1倍；新提出的"实现边缘稳定性"框架消除了优化器依赖的系统偏移，提供了一致且更准确的稳定性阈值预测，并揭示了优化器在时间与空间预算之间主动平衡的作用。

Conclusion: 经典的基于最大Hessian特征值的边缘稳定性理论不够充分，需要以优化器实际更新方向为导向重新定义稳定性阈值。新的"实现边缘稳定性"框架不仅提高了预测准确性，还解锁了新的诊断工具，帮助理解不同优化器在训练动力学中的独特机理。

Abstract: The edge of stability refers to a phenomenon in deep learning with gradient-based optimizers where the Hessian eigenvalues of the loss remain stable above a threshold that the classical descent lemma predicts to be unstable. Previous works formulate the edge of stability with respect to the maximum Hessian eigenvalue and the learning rate. However, we observe that many first-order methods, including gradient descent, significantly violate the stability bound predicted by these theories by a factor as large as $\times 21.1$. Moreover, this deviation turns out to be systematic and highly dependent on the underlying optimizer, which is not captured by previous formulations. This calls for a new formulation of the stability threshold, which we derive from the directional Hessian and the gradient-alignment score with respect to the actual update taken by the optimizer, rather than the maximum curvature mode. Our new formulation of the realized edge of stability not only removes optimizer-dependent offsets and provides more consistent predictions of the stability threshold, but also introduces new diagnostic tools that reveal the unique role of the optimizer in actively balancing between the temporal and spatial budgets in first-order optimization.

</details>


### [23] [Mechanistic Interpretability of Structure-Aware Numerical Reasoning in LLaMA 3.1 8B](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18419)
*Rahul Chowdhury,Timothy A Rupprecht,Senhao Cao,Jiahao Liu,Octavia Camps,David Bau,Pu Zhao,Yanzhi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从机制可解释性角度研究Llama 3.1-8B在数值序列建模中的内部机制，发现模型通过计算和存储一阶差分，并利用类似归纳电路的机制检索相关差分后加到当前值上，从而完成序列预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然LLM在时间序列预测上表现出色且具备上下文学习能力，但其完成数值序列任务的内部机制尚不明确，特别是模型是否真正理解序列底层结构（至少需要对序列中的一阶差分进行推理）。

Method: 设计了一个无法通过短视策略解决的序列建模任务（采样n个随机数并带偏移重复），并从机制可解释性角度进行分析：1) 探针实验(probing)检测模型内部表征中是否计算并存储一阶差分；2) 激活补丁(activation patching)进行反事实分析，定位模型如何检索相关差分并将其加到当前值。

Result: Llama在任务上表现优异，能捕捉底层结构；探针实验表明模型在没有显式监督的情况下在内部表征中计算和存储一阶差分；激活补丁实验揭示模型通过类似归纳电路的机制检索相关一阶差分并加到当前值上。

Conclusion: 该研究首次在LLM中识别出"概念归纳"(concept induction)这一形式，表明LLM在时间序列预测中确实在内部进行结构推理（基于一阶差分），而非仅依赖表面模式匹配。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that large language models (LLMs) exhibit strong numerical sequence modeling capabilities and show promise in time-series prediction. While LLMs display in-context learning capabilities, the mechanisms with which they accomplish time-series prediction remain unclear. Specifically, whether they truly understand the underlying structure, which at a minimum requires reasoning over first differences in the sequence of numbers. To study this, we investigate Llama 3.1-8B from a mechanistic interpretability point of view. Mechanistic interpretability is an emerging field concerned with the reverse engineering of the algorithms learned by neural networks such as LLMs. To assess Llamas' numerical sequence modeling capabilities and to facilitate our mechanistic interpretability analysis, we create a sequence modeling task that cannot be solved without picking up structural cues. Specifically, we sample n random numbers and repeat them with an offset. We find that Llama displays strong performance on our tasks suggesting that it can pick up on the underlying structure. To understand the mechanisms that allow it to do so, we perform probing experiments and activation patching based counterfactual analysis. Probing reveals that the model computes and stores first differences in its internal representations without explicit supervision, indicating that it tracks structural information about the sequence. Activation patching reveals that Llama retrieves the relevant first-difference with a mechanism similar to an induction circuit and subsequently adds it to the current value. Notably, our work represents one of the first studies to identify this form of concept induction in LLMs.

</details>


### [24] [Adaptive Multi-Agent Feature Selection for Personalized Fall Risk Prevention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18450)
*Chang Liu,Ladda Thiamwong,Yanjie Fu,Rui Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PAFIR框架，将老年人跌倒风险预测中的自适应特征选择建模为强化学习问题，在纵向多模态健康数据上联合建模结构依赖与时序动态，实现个性化、随时间演化的特征选择，并在PEER集群随机试验数据上验证了其优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 老年人跌倒风险受多种风险域之间复杂、时变的交互影响，现有方法多为静态模型，无法在跨模态、跨时间维度上自适应建模不断演化的个体化风险因素，且跌倒事件稀疏且延迟的特点进一步增加了风险因素识别的难度。

Method: PAFIR将自适应特征选择形式化为强化学习问题：联合建模评估变量间的结构依赖关系与可穿戴设备物理活动数据的时序动态，利用稀疏跌倒结局信号产生的奖励信号，在重复研究访视中学习自适应特征选择策略，实现个体化、随时间动态调整的特征选择。

Result: 在PEER集群随机对照试验数据上的实验表明，PAFIR相比现有最优基线方法能更有效地捕捉特征相关性的纵向和结构模式，并实现动态的、个体化的特征选择。

Conclusion: 通过随时间自适应调整所选特征，PAFIR能够支持更及时、更个性化的跌倒预防策略，为动态纵向临床数据中的自适应风险建模提供了一种有效范式。

Abstract: Falls among older adults represent a major public health challenge driven by complex, time-varying interactions across multiple risk domains. Effective fall risk factor identification requires learning from heterogeneous longitudinal data while accounting for sparse and delayed fall-related outcome events. However, existing approaches are largely static and fail to adaptively model evolving, individualized risk factors across modalities and time. We propose PAFIR, a Personalized and Adaptive Feature selection framework for fall risk Identification and pRevention, which formulates adaptive feature selection as a reinforcement learning problem over longitudinal multimodal health data. PAFIR jointly models structural dependencies among correlated assessment variables and temporal dynamics in wearable-derived physical activity data, and learns adaptive selection policies across repeated study visits using reward signals derived from sparse fall incidence outcomes. We apply PAFIR to data from the Physio fEedback Exercise pRogram (PEER) cluster-randomized trial. Experimental results demonstrate that PAFIR more effectively captures longitudinal and structural patterns of feature relevance than state-of-the-art baselines, and enables dynamic, subject-specific feature selection. By adapting selected features over time, PAFIR supports more timely and personalized fall prevention strategies.

</details>


### [25] [Atrial Fibrillation Detection with Arbitrary Leads via a Codebook-Based Reconstruction-Classification Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18451)
*Hongtao Li,Jia Wei,Guoyao Li,Yuchen Lei,Guangnian Ma,Jia Xiao,Yuanjun Lai,Shuzhen Lv,Xueqiang Ouyang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了DCGCNet，一种端到端的向量量化变分自编码器，通过局部-全局对比模块和自适应码本向量量化器实现房颤检测与ECG重建，在七个跨数据集场景中AUC均超过0.98，并在噪声条件下保持高诊断精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实临床环境中房颤检测面临导联配置多变、跨数据集域偏移以及生理性和技术性伪影等挑战，现有方法在这些条件下鲁棒性和泛化能力不足，需要开发一个强鲁棒且可泛化的深度学习模型。

Method: 提出DCGCNet，一种端到端向量量化变分自编码器，同时执行AF分类和ECG重建。包含两个关键组件：(1)局部-全局对比模块，学习噪声不变表征；(2)自适应码本向量量化器，动态细化码本原型以对齐输入数据分布，防止码本坍缩并增强泛化能力。

Result: 在标准数据集内12导联评估中达到最先进性能；在七个不同跨数据集场景中一致达到AUC>0.98；在基线漂移、电源线干扰和EMG伪影等现实噪声条件下保持高诊断准确率。

Conclusion: DCGCNet为鲁棒、可泛化和抗噪的房颤检测建立了新的基准，展示了在真实临床环境中部署的潜力。

Abstract: \textbf{Background and Objective}: Reliable atrial fibrillation (AF) detection from electrocardiogram (ECG) signals remains challenging in real-world clinical settings due to variable lead configurations, cross-dataset domain shifts, and pervasive physiological and technical artifacts. So we develop a robust and generalizable deep learning model for accurate AF detection.\\ \textbf{Methods}: We propose the Dual-Codebook Graph Collaborative Network (DCGCNet), a novel end-to-end vector-quantized variational autoencoder that jointly performs AF classification and ECG reconstruction. DCGCNet introduces two key components: (1) a Local-Global Contrastive Module for learning noise-invariant representations, and (2) an Adaptive Codebook Vector Quantizer that dynamically refines codebook prototypes to better align with input data distributions, thereby preventing codebook collapse and enhancing generalization.\\ \textbf{Results}: DCGCNet achieves state-of-the-art performance in standard intra-dataset 12-lead evaluation and demonstrates exceptional cross-dataset generalization across seven diverse settings, consistently attaining AUC > 0.98 in all cases. Furthermore, it maintains high diagnostic accuracy under realistic noisy conditions, including baseline wander, powerline interference, and EMG artifacts.\\ \textbf{Conclusions}: DCGCNet establishes a new benchmark for robust, generalizable, and noise-resilient AF detection, showing strong potential for deployment in real-world clinical environments.

</details>


### [26] [ERASE: EaRly bAckpropagation SchEdule for Faster Training of Modern Recommendation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18469)
*Ergan Shang,Flavio Sales Truzzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ERASE方法，通过将Forward-Forward算法的解耦机制重新解释为调度原语，在单独的CUDA流上提前启动每个解耦子图的反向传播，使其与后续前向计算重叠执行，从而在不牺牲模型质量的前提下提升轻量级代理模型在大规模加速器上的训练吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻量级代理模型因其较小的kernel常导致现代加速器利用率不足；传统训练将前向和后向规划为不连续阶段，空余的计算能力无法被另一阶段的工作填充，造成资源浪费。

Method: 将Forward-Forward的解耦机制重新诠释为调度原语：给定局部目标后，解耦块的输出移除下游梯度依赖，使其反向传播在前向完成后立即可用。ERASE在单独的CUDA流上提前启动每个解耦子图的反向传播，与后续前向工作重叠执行。

Result: 在执行轨迹上验证了轻量级transformer中的重叠效果及其限制（kernel饱和设备时无并发容量）；在大型点击率预估模型上，解耦六个稠密子架构使训练吞吐量提升最高达9.51%，同时归一化熵接近基线。

Conclusion: ERASE通过前反向重叠调度有效利用加速器空余能力，在大型模型中实现了可观的训练吞吐量提升，且不损害模型性能指标。

Abstract: Lightweight proxy models enable rapid experimentation without repeatedly training frontier-scale systems, but their small kernels often leave modern accelerators underutilized. Conventional training compounds this inefficiency by scheduling the forward and backward passes as disjoint phases, so spare capacity in one cannot be filled by work from the other. We reinterpret the detachment mechanism of Forward-Forward (FF) as a scheduling primitive: given a local objective, detaching a block's output removes downstream gradient dependencies, making its backward pass ready when its forward pass finishes. ERASE launches each detached subgraph's backward pass early on a separate CUDA stream, overlapping it with subsequent forward work. Execution trace on a lightweight transformer demonstrates this overlap and its limit: a kernel that saturates the device leaves no capacity for concurrency. On a large-scale click-through-rate model, detaching six dense subarchitectures improves training throughput by up to $9.51\%$ while keeping normalized entropy close to the baseline.

</details>


### [27] [Physics-Unrolled Neural Operator for Wireless Field Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18495)
*Rafid Umayer Murshed,Saif Ur Rahman,Mingyue Tang,Elahe Soltanaghai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PU-HNO是一种三阶段级联的物理展开混合神经算子，用于从低保真射线追踪输出和场景先验中预测高保真室内无线电地图，逐步捕获反射、衍射和散射效应。理论证明在条件无偏标签噪声下，模型可学习稳定传播结构并超越自身训练标签；实验表明其在图像质量和无线部署指标上均优于图像到图像基线、无线学习模型和单体神经算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真无线电地图的精细空间细节由复杂传播效应决定，精确仿真成本高昂；机器学习虽有前景，但大规模高质量训练标签的生成同样困难——负担得起的标签来自有限射线仿真，虽比低保真输入丰富，却带有残余蒙特卡罗噪声。

Method: 提出PU-HNO（Physics-Unrolled Hybrid Neural Operator），一种三阶段级联架构，从低保真射线追踪输出和场景先验出发，通过逐步捕获反射、衍射和散射效应来预测高保真室内无线电地图，而非将无线电地图视为通用图像。

Result: 在多样化的楼层平面图实验中，PU-HNO在图像质量和无线部署指标两方面均优于图像到图像基线、无线学习模型和单体神经算子；同时理论上证明了在条件无偏标签噪声下模型能学习稳定传播结构并超越自身训练标签。

Conclusion: PU-HNO有效解决了高保真无线电地图预测中标签噪声问题，通过物理展开的级联设计显著提升了预测精度，验证了面向物理结构设计模型相比通用图像处理方法在无线场景中的优势。

Abstract: Radio maps are essential for wireless decision-making tasks such as access-point placement, coverage planning, and localization, but their fine spatial details are governed by complex propagation effects and are costly to simulate accurately. Machine learning offers a path to high-fidelity radio-map prediction without running expensive high-fidelity simulations for every scene. However, generating high-quality training labels at scale is also difficult: the affordable labels come from finite-ray simulations, which are richer than low-fidelity inputs but carry residual Monte Carlo noise. We address this challenge with Physics-Unrolled Hybrid Neural Operator (PU-HNO), a three-stage cascade that predicts high-fidelity indoor radio maps from low-fidelity ray-tracing outputs and scene priors by progressively capturing reflection, diffraction, and scattering effects, rather than treating radio maps as generic images. We prove that, under conditionally unbiased label noise, the model can learn stable propagation structure and outperform its own training labels. Experiments across diverse floorplans show that PU-HNO outperforms image-to-image baselines, wireless learning models, and monolithic neural operators across both image-quality and wireless deployment metrics.

</details>


### [28] [Tianmu-TC: Physics-constraints Generative Artificial Intelligence for Global Tropical Cyclone Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18500)
*Shiqi Zhang,Pan Mu,Cheng Huang,Hanting Yan,Yuchao Zhu,Jinglin Zhang,Shengyong Chen,Shoujuan Shu,Cong Bai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Tianmu-TC，一种基于物理约束的生成式框架，用于全球热带气旋预测。该模型在西太平洋数据上训练，通过物理约束生成可控输出以降低不确定性，在多个全球洋盆上优于确定性/集合AI模型和ECMWF等权威NWP系统，且计算成本显著更低，在数据稀疏、异常路径、快速增强和减弱等挑战性场景中表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热带气旋（TC）造成的强风和强降雨风险严重，但其路径和强度预测仍具有挑战性，原因在于大气的混沌特性及初始条件误差的快速放大导致预测不确定性增加。现有数值天气预报（NWP）和深度学习模型虽然取得进展，但计算成本高且常在复杂气象场景下失效，亟需更可靠且高效的预测方法。

Method: 提出Tianmu-TC，一种物理约束的生成式框架。该模型在西太平洋数据上训练，利用物理约束生成可控输出，从而降低预测不确定性并提升可靠性。该框架属于生成式AI方法，结合了物理约束来确保预测结果符合气象规律。

Result: 实验表明Tianmu-TC在全球洋盆预测中优于确定性及集合气象AI模型和ECMWF等权威NWP系统，同时计算成本显著更低。此外，该模型在数据稀疏、异常路径、快速增强和快速减弱等挑战性场景中均表现出良好性能。

Conclusion: 物理约束的生成式AI为全球热带气旋预测提供了一条有前景的路径，能够实现可靠且高效的预测，为未来气象预报人工智能的发展提供了新方向。

Abstract: Tropical cyclones (TCs) pose severe risks from strong winds and heavy rainfall. However, forecasting their track and intensity remains challenging due to chaotic atmosphere and the rapid amplification of initial condition errors, leading to growing forecast uncertainty. While numerical weather prediction (NWP) and deep learning models have made progress, they remain computationally demanding and often fail under complex meteorological scenarios. Here, we present Tianmu-TC, a physics-constraints generative framework for global TC forecasting. Trained on Western North Pacific data, Tianmu-TC leverages physics-constraints to generate controllable outputs with reduced uncertainty thus improving forecast reliability. Experiments show Tianmu-TC outperforms deterministic and ensemble meteorological artificial intelligence models and authoritative NWP systems such as ECMWF in global ocean basins, with significantly lower computational cost. We further show Tianmu-TC performs well in challenging scenarios such as data sparsity, anomaly tracks, rapid intensification and weakening. These findings suggest physics-constraints generative AI offers a promising approach for reliable, efficient global TC forecasting.

</details>


### [29] [LLM-Powered Predictive Decision-Making for Sustainable Data Center Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18503)
*Hanzhao Wang,Jingxuan Wu,Yumeng Li,Yu Pan,Guanting Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于大语言模型的预测性调度系统，通过LLM预测代码的执行时间和能耗等指标，结合实时调度算法优化GPU资源分配，在真实数据中心实现了32%的能耗降低和30%的等待时间减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI驱动的工作负载（尤其是大语言模型）在数据中心中的快速增长带来了显著的能源和资源消耗问题，亟需在保证运营效率的同时降低环境影响的调度方案。

Method: 利用LLM从源代码中预测执行时间和能耗等关键指标，并可扩展至水耗、碳排放等可持续性指标；预测模型之后连接一个实时调度算法，以优化能源消耗和排队延迟为目标分配GPU资源。

Result: 与真实数据中心合作验证，实现了32%的能耗削减和30%的等待时间减少。系统具备快速推理、跨任务泛化能力以及低训练数据需求。

Conclusion: 该框架为AI驱动的数据中心基础设施提供了一种实用且高效的可持续性调度方案，展示了在推进AI基础设施可持续性目标方面的强大潜力。

Abstract: The growing demand for AI-driven workloads, particularly from Large Language Models (LLMs), has raised concerns about the significant energy and resource consumption in data centers. This work introduces a novel LLM-based predictive scheduling system designed to enhance operational efficiency while reducing the environmental impact of data centers. Our system utilizes an LLM to predict key metrics such as execution time and energy consumption from source code, and it has the potential to extend to other sustainability-focused metrics like water usage for cooling and carbon emissions, provided the data center can track such data. The predictive model is followed by a real-time scheduling algorithm that allocates GPU resources, aiming to improve sustainability by optimizing both energy consumption and queuing delays. With fast inference times, the ability to generalize across diverse task types, and minimal data requirements for training, our approach offers a practical solution for data center scheduling. This framework demonstrates strong potential for advancing sustainability objectives in AI-driven infrastructure. Through our collaboration with a data center, we achieved a 32% reduction in energy consumption and a 30% decrease in waiting time.

</details>


### [30] [Evaluating and Explaining Prompt Sensitivity of LLMs Using Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18539)
*Ruiyang Qin,Qingzhuo Wang,Tian Wang,Zhihua Wei,Wen Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于交互（interactions）的细粒度分析方法来研究大语言模型的提示敏感性，并引入IPS指标量化提示微小变化时交互的变动，通过对50个开源LLM的分析，发现监督微调、增大模型规模、密集架构和少样本学习能降低提示敏感性，且其共同机制是减少低阶交互的提示敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通过比较提示变化时LLM的最终输出来评估提示敏感性，这种粗粒度指标无法解释提示敏感性的内在原因。作者希望引入更细粒度的工具来深入理解并解释提示敏感性的根源。

Method: 将LLM的输出分数分解为一组交互（interactions），每个交互表示一组输入变量之间的非线性关系。通过量化输入提示发生微小变化时交互的改变，提出交互式提示敏感性（IPS）指标。将该指标应用于50个开源LLM进行系统分析。

Result: 发现即使LLM输出相同，细微的提示变化也可能触发交互的严重不稳定。识别出四种能降低提示敏感性的因素：监督微调、更大模型规模、密集架构和少样本学习。更关键的是，发现这四个因素降低提示敏感性的共同机制，即它们都倾向于减少低阶交互（涉及少量输入变量的交互）的提示敏感性。

Conclusion: 交互分析为LLM提示敏感性提供了比输出比较更深层的解释，揭示了降低提示敏感性的关键因素及其共享的内在机制，为构建更稳定的LLM提供了理论指导。

Abstract: The remarkable capabilities of large language models (LLMs) are often undermined by their instability. Even subtle and semantically irrelevant changes in prompts can cause dramatic fluctuations in performance, a phenomenon known as prompt sensitivity. Previous studies typically evaluate prompt sensitivity by comparing the LLM's final outputs when prompts change. However, such coarse-grained metrics fail to explain the internal reasons for prompt sensitivity. In this paper, we introduce interactions as a fine-grained tool to analyze prompt sensitivity of LLMs. Specifically, we decompose the output score of the LLM into a set of interactions. Each interaction represents a nonlinear relationship involving a set of input variables. We discover that subtle changes to prompts can trigger severe instability in interactions, even when the outputs of the LLM remain the same. To this end, we propose an Interaction-based Prompt Sensitivity (IPS) metric by quantifying changes in interactions when we introduce subtle changes to prompts. We apply the IPS metric to 50 open-source LLMs and uncover four factors that reduce the prompt sensitivity of LLMs, including supervised fine-tuning, increased model scales, dense architectures, and few-shot learning. More crucially, we discover a common mechanism by which these four factors reduce prompt sensitivity: all four factors tend to reduce the prompt sensitivity of low-order interactions (i.e., interactions involving few input variables).

</details>


### [31] [MARCUS: Missing-Aware Region Representation with Contextual Urban Signals for Rent Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18546)
*Chenya Huang,Bin Liang,Zhidong Li,Yuxi Lu,Kunqi Li,Justin Wang,Fang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MARCUS，一种将数据缺失视为语义信号而非噪声的城市区域表示学习模型，通过三阶段机制（Intra Learning、Inter Learning、Fusion）建模缺失模式，在悉尼和纽约的租金预测任务上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态城市数据在区域表示学习中广泛应用，但数据不完整性问题突出。现有方法将缺失当作噪声进行插补处理，忽略了缺失本身可能蕴含的语义价值。作者认为缺失模式应当被视为一种有价值的上下文城市信号来建模。

Method: MARCUS采用三阶段建模策略：(1) Intra Learning：联合编码观测特征与缺失模式；(2) Inter Learning：估计各模态的可靠性以指导跨模态交互；(3) Fusion：使用缺失感知和时间感知的门控机制生成最终区域嵌入表示。

Result: 在悉尼和纽约的真实数据集上应用于租金预测任务，MARCUS达到最优性能，与最佳基线相比，MAE在悉尼降低51.35%，在纽约降低12.62%。额外的基于插补的消融实验和随机额外缺失分析进一步验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 将数据缺失视为具有语义价值的上下文信号而非纯噪声，能够显著提升多模态城市区域表示学习的性能，特别是在具有长期趋势和季节波动的租金预测任务中表现突出。

Abstract: Multimodal urban data has expanded the applications of urban region representation learning, such as functional zone identification and real estate appraisal, but also introduces challenges caused by data incompleteness. Existing studies usually handle missing data through imputation, treating missingness as noise while ignoring its potential semantic value. To address this issue, we propose MARCUS, a missing-aware region representation model that treats missingness as a contextual urban signal. MARCUS models missingness in three stages: Intra Learning jointly encodes observed features and missing patterns, Inter Learning estimates modality reliability to guide cross-modal interaction, and Fusion uses missing-aware and time-aware gating to generate the final region embedding. We apply MARCUS to rent prediction, a task with long-term trends and seasonal fluctuations, using real-world datasets from Sydney and New York. Experimental results show that MARCUS achieves state-of-the-art performance, reducing MAE by 51.35% on Sydney and 12.62% on New York compared with the best baselines. Additional experiments, including an imputation-based ablation study and randomized additional-missingness analysis, further demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [32] [Performance Drift Detection in Machine Learning as a Service (MLaaS) for IoT Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18555)
*Deepak Kanneganti,Sajib Mistry,Sheik Mohammad Mostakim Fattah,Erik Elmroth,Aneesh Krishna,Monowar Bhuyan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向IoT环境的MLaaS性能漂移检测框架，通过提取模型学习服务行为、联合捕获输入数据和MLaaS行为变化，并设计自适应时序漂移检测机制来动态调整监控频率，在真实数据集上实现了显著的检测性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT环境的动态性导致数据分布频繁变化，影响MLaaS稳定性；周期性更新引入性能漂移。由于MLaaS客户端作为黑盒用户无法访问内部数据和参数，漂移检测极具挑战性，而现有方法难以有效解决这一问题。

Method: 提出三部分组成的MLaaS性能漂移检测框架：(1)利用MLaaS提取模型从输入-输出对中学习服务行为并识别影响预测的特征；(2)设计MPDD模型联合捕获输入数据和MLaaS行为变化；(3)提出APDDM自适应-时序性能漂移检测机制，基于行为和数据的变异程度动态调整监控频率，实现及时漂移检测。

Result: 在真实数据集上的大量实验表明：MPDD相比基线漂移检测方法实现22-25%的精度提升；APDDM相比固定间隔监控平均精度提升约4%，漏检率降低约9%。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架有效解决了MLaaS在IoT场景下黑盒驱动的性能漂移检测难题，通过自适应的监控机制实现了更及时、更准确的漂移感知，为MLaaS服务管理提供了有力支撑。

Abstract: Machine Learning as a Service (MLaaS) is a powerful cloud paradigm enabling data-driven intelligent applications in Internet of Things (IoT) environments, widely adopted across healthcare, smart homes, and industry due to its cost-effectiveness. However, the dynamic nature of IoT frequently alters data distributions, affecting MLaaS stability, while periodic MLaaS updates further introduce performance drift. Unlike traditional ML systems, MLaaS clients operate as black-box users without access to internal data or parameters, making drift detection particularly challenging. To address this, we propose a novel MLaaS Performance Drift Detection framework for IoT environments. The framework first employs an MLaaS extraction model that learns service behavior from input-output pairs and identifies prediction-influenced features. Building on this, the proposed MLaaS Performance Drift Detection (MPDD) model jointly captures variations in input data and MLaaS behavior. We further design an Adaptive-Temporal Performance Drift Detection Mechanism (APDDM) that dynamically adjusts monitoring frequency based on behavioral and data variations, enabling timely drift detection for effective service management. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that MPDD achieves up to 22-25% accuracy improvement over baseline drift detection methods. APDDM provides an average accuracy gain of approximately 4% and reduces the miss detection rate by around 9% compared to fixed-interval monitoring.

</details>


### [33] [MorphoGP: A Nonparametric Framework for Predicting Equilibrium Beach Profiles Under Tidal Influence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18558)
*Xi Wu,Yanqing Wei,Hang Yin,Pengze Li,Hongshuai Qi,Xi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MorphoGP，一个基于类别的统一高斯过程框架，用于在潮汐影响下预测平衡海滩剖面（EBP）。框架通过对比学习的ContourCluster自动分类潮汐影响的海滩形态，每个类别内由专门的高斯过程专家学习波浪、潮汐、沉积物等环境描述符与海滩剖面形状之间的统计关联，最后通过Gating Net以概率加权方式整合各专家输出。在包含180多个中国潮汐影响海岸海滩剖面的数据集上，MorphoGP相比最优基线将测试RMSE降低了约59.3%，最终RMSE达0.297米。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潮汐影响下平衡海滩剖面的预测对海岸可持续发展、岸线保护和生态系统管理至关重要，但波浪、潮汐和沉积过程之间的高度非线性相互作用使其极具挑战性。传统经验模型和数值模型在不同海岸环境中的适应性有限，特别是在潮汐过程重要的海滩系统中表现尤为不足，亟需更有效的数据驱动预测方法。

Method: MorphoGP采用三步框架：(1) 基于对比学习的ContourCluster模型自动分类潮汐影响的海滩形态；(2) 在每个形态类别内，训练专门的高斯过程专家，学习波浪、潮汐、沉积物等环境描述符与海滩剖面形状之间的统计关联；(3) 通过Gating Net以概率加权机制整合所有专家的输出，生成最终预测。

Result: 在中国海岸超过180个潮汐影响海滩剖面的数据上进行评估，MorphoGP相较于传统和深度学习模型均取得了更优的预测性能，相比最佳基线将测试RMSE降低了约59.3%，最终RMSE达到0.297米。

Conclusion: MorphoGP为潮汐影响下的平衡海滩剖面预测提供了一种物理信息引导的、数据驱动的工具，可服务于海岸管理。但必须指出，在过程层面的更强物理耦合仍然是未来研究的重要方向。

Abstract: The prediction of equilibrium beach profiles under tidal influence is of fundamental importance for sustainable coastal development, informing shoreline protection strategies and managing coastal ecosystems under changing environmental conditions. However, it remains challenging due to the highly nonlinear interactions among wave, tide, and sedimentary processes. Traditional empirical and numerical models often exhibit limited adaptability across diverse coastal environments, with especially pronounced limitations in beach systems where tidal processes are important . To improve data-driven prediction under these conditions, this study proposes MorphoGP, a unified category-specific Gaussian process framework for predicting equilibrium beach profiles (EBPs) under tidal influence. The framework first introduces a ContourCluster model based on contrastive learning to classify tide-influenced beach morphologies automatically. Within each morphological category, a specialized Gaussian process expert learns statistical associations between environmental descriptors including waves, tides, and sediments and the beach profile's shape. A Gating Net then integrates the outputs of all experts through a probabilistic weighting mechanism to produce the final prediction. Evaluated on data from over 180 beach profiles from tide-influenced coasts along the Chinese coast, MorphoGP achieves improved predictive performance compared with conventional and deep learning models, reducing the test RMSE by about 59.3\% compared with the best baseline and achieving a final RMSE of 0.297 m. The proposed framework provides a physically informed, data-driven tool for equilibrium beach-profile prediction under tidal influence and coastal management, while stronger process-level physical coupling remains an important direction for future development.

</details>


### [34] [NanoSleep: A Parameter-Efficient Hybrid Temporal Convolutional Network for Single-Channel Sleep Stage Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18571)
*S M Asif Hossain,Shruti Kshirsagar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NanoSleep是一个紧凑的混合时序卷积网络，用于单通道EEG睡眠分期分类，在保证高精度的同时显著减小模型体积，适合资源受限的可穿戴设备和家庭睡眠监测场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度学习睡眠分期模型虽然准确率高，但模型庞大，难以部署在资源受限的设备（如可穿戴设备和家庭监测设备）上，需要在精度与效率之间取得平衡。

Method: NanoSleep采用四个核心模块：1) 可学习的Sinc卷积前端；2) 融合多尺度时序与频谱表示的双分支特征提取器；3) 带通道重校准的门控膨胀时序卷积主干网络；4) 用于序列级解码的条件随机场（CRF）。此外，使用加权校准焦点损失函数解决类别不平衡问题。

Result: 在Sleep-EDF和Sleep-EDF-Expanded数据集上采用受试者独立交叉验证，NanoSleep一致优于六种代表性基线方法；消融实验确认了每个主要组件的贡献。

Conclusion: NanoSleep在精度与效率之间实现了有效平衡，非常适合可穿戴设备、家庭睡眠监测以及资源受限的临床应用场景。

Abstract: Sleep stage classification from single-channel electroencephalography (EEG) is essential for wearable and home-based sleep monitoring. However, many deep learning models achieve high accuracy at the cost of large model sizes, which limits their deployment on resource-constrained devices. In this work, we present NanoSleep, a compact hybrid temporal convolutional network for automatic sleep stage classification. NanoSleep combines a learnable Sinc-convolutional front end, a dual-branch feature extractor that fuses multi-scale temporal and spectral representations, a gated dilated temporal convolutional backbone with channel recalibration, and a conditional random field for sequence-level decoding. We further employ a weighted calibrated focal loss to address class imbalance. We evaluate NanoSleep on the Sleep-EDF and Sleep-EDF-Expanded datasets using subject-wise cross-validation. The proposed model consistently outperforms six representative baseline methods, and an ablation study confirms the contribution of each major component. These results demonstrate that NanoSleep provides an effective balance between accuracy and efficiency, making it well suited for wearable devices, home-based sleep monitoring, and resource-constrained clinical applications.

</details>


### [35] [Continual Reasoning Gym: Diagnosing and Harnessing Shared Reasoning in Continual RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18574)
*Lirui Luo,Guoxi Zhang,Hongming Xu,Rongqing Li,Cong Fang,Lifeng Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究持续RLVR（基于可验证奖励的强化学习）在多任务场景下的能力扩展问题，提出Continual Reasoning Gym环境，发现顺序RLVR存在适度遗忘但最终性能低于联合训练（MTRL），其差距并非仅由遗忘造成，而是与共享推理结构有关。据此提出Continual Prompt Replay (CPR)方法，通过重放先前任务的提示并用当前策略重新生成响应，利用共享推理来提升新任务与未来任务的学习，最终在平均性能上达到MTRL水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR通常在多任务上联合后训练推理模型，但当新任务不断加入时，重新运行多任务RLVR代价高昂。因此需要研究持续RLVR，即在每个任务到达时增量更新现有模型。核心问题在于这种方式能否达到与联合训练相当的性能，这关乎持续学习范式在推理模型上的可行性。

Method: 1) 构建Continual Reasoning Gym，一个持续RLVR环境，将文本和视觉推理任务组织为五个任务序列；2) 通过实验识别两个关键观察：顺序RLVR的遗忘程度适中，但最终性能低于MTRL；将最终性能分解后发现遗忘只解释了部分差距；3) 提出共享推理概念，指出可迁移的推理结构使训练一个任务平均上有助于其他任务；4) 基于此提出Continual Prompt Replay (CPR)，通过重放先前任务的提示并用当前策略重新生成响应（而非存储旧响应），来利用共享推理改善当前及未来任务的学习。

Result: 在Continual Reasoning Gym的五个任务序列上，CPR方法在平均性能上达到了MTRL（多任务联合训练）的水平，而其他持续学习方法均未达到该水平，验证了CPR利用共享推理的有效性。

Conclusion: 研究表明，顺序RLVR的最终性能差距并非单纯由遗忘导致，而主要源于方法层面的不足；共享推理是持续RLVR可被利用的关键机制，CPR通过基于当前策略重新生成回放响应，成功弥合了持续RLVR与多任务联合训练之间的性能差距，为大规模推理模型的能力持续扩展提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) commonly post-trains reasoning models on multiple tasks, while rerunning multitask RLVR (MTRL) as new tasks are added makes capability expansion costly. We therefore study continual RLVR, which updates the existing model as each task arrives. The central question is whether a model updated this way can perform as well as a jointly trained model. To answer this question, we introduce Continual Reasoning Gym, a continual-RLVR environment that organizes text and visual reasoning tasks into five task sequences. In this setting, we identify two key observations: Sequential RLVR exhibits modest forgetting, yet its final performance remains below that of MTRL. To understand the latter, we decompose final performance and show that forgetting accounts for only part of the gap. To explain the former, we identify shared reasoning: transferable reasoning structure allows training on one task to support others on average. We therefore introduce Continual Prompt Replay (CPR), which harnesses shared reasoning to improve learning on the arriving and future tasks by replaying previous-task prompts and regenerating their responses with the current policy. On average, only CPR reaches MTRL-level performance.

</details>


### [36] [Beyond receptive fields: sequence-pooled normalization can supply most of a sequence labeler's context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18576)
*Qing Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了一个反直觉的现象：卷积序列标注器的感受野通常被视为有效上下文的边界，但当归一化层在推理时从当前输入沿序列维度计算统计量时，这些统计量会打开一条跨越整个序列的路径，绕过卷积感受野提供全局上下文。作者从雅可比矩阵推导了这一结论，并用合成数据和真实基因组数据验证，还发现该路径会严重影响消融实验的归因结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卷积序列标注器的感受野被广泛用作模型可用上下文的度量，它决定膨胀调度、约束流式推理范围并支撑局部性声明。然而作者发现这一假设可能是错误的：归一化层在推理时从当前输入的序列维度计算统计量，会创造一条绕过感受野的全局上下文路径，因此需要重新审视感受野作为上下文边界这一普遍假设的正确性。

Method: 作者通过计算归一化层的雅可比矩阵进行理论推导（无需实验），论证序列跨度统计量会形成一条穿透卷积感受野的全局路径，且该路径所携带的信息具有闭式解。随后在合成标注过程（具有可计算最优解）上进行验证，并通过封闭路径（按位置取相同统计量）进行对照实验，同时在模拟基因组和真实1000 Genomes单倍型数据上测试。

Result: 在合成数据上，仅覆盖9个位置的网络已能达到与全序列最优解相差0.009的性能，而其感受野范围仅相当于接近随机猜测的水平。封闭该路径后，扩大感受野带来的收益最高可提升一个数量级。在真实基因组数据上，该路径还使消融实验对感受野扩展模块的贡献高估了8.3-16.1倍。

Conclusion: 序列跨度归一化本质上可以替代部分感受野的扩展功能，尤其是在标签以长段形式出现时。当标签切换频繁时，这种替代效应减弱。关于感受野的论证和消融实验的数值本身并不算错，但都将功劳归因于错误的组件——实际提供全局信息的是归一化层而非卷积感受野本身。

Abstract: A convolutional sequence labeler's receptive field is routinely treated as the extent of the model's usable context: it sets dilation schedules, bounds streaming horizons, and underwrites locality claims. However, we show that this can be false: when a normalization layer computes statistics from the current input along the sequence at inference, those statistics open a sequence-spanning path that bypasses the convolutional receptive field to provide global context. We derive this from the layer's Jacobian (the criterion needs no experiment), and what the path carries has a closed form. On a synthetic labeling process with computable optima, the global summary that a sequence-spanning normalization encodes already supplies almost all of what a larger receptive field would buy where labels come in long runs: a network reaching 9 positions comes within 0.009 of the whole-sequence optimum, against a near-chance bound for its reach. Closing the path, by taking the same statistics per position, multiplies what enlarging the receptive field is worth by up to an order of magnitude on simulated genomes at every difficulty level tested and on real 1000 Genomes haplotypes. The same path also confounds attribution: ablating a trained network's receptive-field-enlarging blocks severs part of the path, overstating their contribution 8.3-16.1-fold relative to retraining from scratch. The substitution of normalization for receptive field fades as labels switch more often. Where labels run long, neither the receptive-field justification nor the ablation is wrong about its numbers, but both credit the wrong component.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 选择性动力学与其余块变换显著重组了微观弛豫层级，各层级谱并不相同。但完整块发展出可复现的慢模式连续谱，其红外区随序列长度增加而更好分辨。长序列Mamba的指数稳定在β_M≈-0.17，记忆动力学接近边缘1/t行为。Transformer全块谱表现出密切相关的红外组织，代表指数为β_Tr≈-0.1。

Conclusion: 结果表明显式状态空间记忆与集体红外组织是可以分离的，不同的序列架构能够发展出密切相关的近边缘慢模式动力学。这扩展了红外集体组织在Transformer之外的普适性，并为认知场理论描述的动力学结构提供了独立检验。

Abstract: Whether distinct neural architectures develop common collective dynamics remains an open question. Recent analysis of Transformer language models revealed a nearly flat, weakly infrared-enhanced time-scale density of states (TDOS) associated with near-marginal long-memory dynamics. Here we test whether a closely related organization emerges in Mamba, whose selective state-space dynamics provides a fundamentally different microscopic mechanism. Mamba allows relaxation dynamics to be resolved at three levels: the intrinsic spectrum of the learned state-space generator, its input-conditioned selective rescaling, and the collective TDOS of the complete block measured from its Jacobian. These spectra are not identical: selective dynamics and the remaining block transformations substantially reorganize the microscopic relaxation hierarchy. Nevertheless, the full block develops a reproducible slow-mode continuum whose infrared sector becomes progressively better resolved with increasing sequence length. Cumulative analysis yields $ρ(λ)\simλ^β$, with the long-sequence Mamba exponent stabilizing near $β_{\rm M}\simeq-0.17$. The corresponding memory dynamics follows $K(t)\sim t^{-(1+β)}$, close to the marginal $1/t$ regime. Despite fundamentally different microscopic dynamics, Transformer full-block spectra exhibit closely related infrared organization, with representative exponents of order $β_{\rm Tr}\sim-0.1$. These results separate explicit state-space memory from collective infrared organization and show that distinct sequence architectures can develop closely related near-marginal slow-mode dynamics. They extend infrared collective organization beyond Transformers and provide an independent test of the dynamical structure described by Cognitive Field Theory.

</details>

*Shuibai Zhang,Xinchi Liu,Fred Zhangzhi Peng,Zhihan Yang,Shutong Wu,Yingzi Ma,Jiawei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了蛋白语言模型在推理时引导（guidance）过程中存在的一种特定失败模式——离流形坍缩（off-manifold collapse），即引导后的激活向量落入与随机氨基酸输入统计上不可区分的区域，导致序列退化但属性评分器仍认为成功。作者提出了一种零训练的后处理步骤——马氏过滤（Mahalanobis filtering），通过密度先验筛选保持自然蛋白激活典型性的候选序列，在不修改生成器的前提下同时提升属性得分和结构合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白语言模型已成为蛋白序列设计的常用先验，推理时引导作为微调的替代方案日益流行，但面临两难：引导力度过轻则几乎不动属性，过强则生成序列难以折叠。现有方法缺乏对这类失败模式的检测手段，且属性oracle可能不仅无法识别失败、甚至会在可溶性优化中主动奖励这种坍缩。

Method: 1) 揭示失败具有特定的、低成本可检测的信号——模型的自身表征发生离流形坍缩，引导激活落到与随机氨基酸输入统计上不可区分的区域。2) 提出马氏过滤（Mahalanobis filtering）：在自然蛋白激活上构建廉价密度先验，仅保留在该先验下仍具典型性的候选序列，作为零训练的后处理步骤，无需修改生成器。

Result: 在匹配的引导设置下，马氏过滤同时提升了保留序列的属性得分和结构合理性，计算成本可忽略，且无需触碰生成器；该方法可跨不同的引导方法迁移。结构（structure）和组成（composition）指标能暴露失败，而属性oracle（尤其溶解度）可能无法识别甚至奖励失败。

Conclusion: 由于失败在候选序列完成时即可见，因此可以从输出端检测而非修改生成器。马氏过滤作为一种训练无关、即插即用的后处理筛选方案，为推理时引导蛋白语言模型提供了一种廉价且有效的安全保障机制。作者开源了激活统计量。

Abstract: Protein language models are widely used priors for protein sequence design, and a growing body of work controls them at inference time as an alternative to fine-tuning. Such guidance faces a dilemma: mild enough to preserve natural activation statistics, it barely moves the property; strong enough to move it, the generations become progressively harder to fold. We show the failure has a specific and cheaply detectable signature, an off-manifold collapse of the model's own representations. Guided activations fall toward a region statistically indistinguishable from random amino-acid input, and the sequences degenerate to low complexity, yet the property oracle being optimized can still score these generations as a success. The optimized oracle can therefore fail to witness the collapse and, for solubility, can actively reward it, whereas structure and composition expose the failure. Because the failure is already visible in a finished candidate, we detect it at the output rather than modify the generator. We introduce a cheap density prior over natural protein activations and keep only the candidates that remain typical under it, a training-free post-hoc step we call Mahalanobis filtering. At matched guidance settings it improves both the property score and the structural plausibility of the sequences it keeps at negligible cost, without touching the generator, and transfers across different guidance methods. We release the activation statistic at https://huggingface.co/Shuibai12138/off-manifold-collapse-plm

</details>


### [39] [Denoising-Aware Inversion: Revealing Privacy Risks in Noise-Protected Text Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18610)
*Yubo Wang,Shujie Cui,James Bailey,Hongzhi Yin,Wenyu Liang,Min Tang,Shiyue Qin,Weiqing Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了噪声保护下的文本嵌入反转攻击问题，指出现有生成式反转方法在仅能观察带噪嵌入时因"双重噪声陷阱"而失效，并提出DAEI（去噪感知嵌入反转管道），通过使用Stein无偏风险估计的无监督残差去噪自编码器与生成式文本反转相结合，在BLEU上相对提升154%，挑战了高斯扰动足以防止嵌入隐私泄露的普遍假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本嵌入虽然紧凑且语义丰富，但嵌入反转攻击可泄露原始文本的大量隐私信息。高斯噪声扰动是常用的防御手段，但现有研究未深入考察噪声保护嵌入在自适应攻击者面前的安全性，因此需要验证噪声防御是否真的足够安全。

Method: 提出DAEI管道，包含：(1) 残差去噪自编码器，使用Stein无偏风险估计(SURE)进行无监督训练，仅从带噪观测中学习去噪；(2) 将去噪后的嵌入输入生成式文本反转模型进行文本重建。同时分析了"双重噪声陷阱"这一导致现有方法在噪声保护设置下失效的根本原因。

Result: DAEI在BLEU上相对现有生成式反转基线提升约154%，token级F1和ROUGE-L提升32-60%，证明了在噪声保护设置下仍能实现高质量的嵌入反转。

Conclusion: 高斯噪声扰动不能被视为足够安全的嵌入隐私防御手段，自适应攻击者仍可通过去噪感知的反转方法恢复原始文本的实质信息，这对嵌入表示的隐私保护设计提出了新的挑战。

Abstract: Dense text embeddings are widely used in data mining, retrieval, and downstream machine learning systems due to their compact and semantically rich representations, but recent embedding inversion attacks have shown that they can expose substantial information about the original text, leading to serious privacy leakage risks. A common defense is to release perturbed embeddings by adding Gaussian noise, which is simple yet effective against standard inversion attacks and does not significantly degrade embedding utility for downstream tasks. However, it remains unclear whether such noise-protected embeddings are sufficiently safe against adaptive attackers that explicitly account for the perturbation process. In this paper, we study text embedding inversion in a noise-protected setting, where the attacker can observe only noisy embeddings and has no access to clean embedding targets. We first analyze why existing generative inversion methods fail under this setting and identify a "Double Noise Trap", which fundamentally prevents standard generative inversion models from achieving high-quality reconstruction. To address this challenge, we propose DAEI, a denoising-aware embedding inversion pipeline that combines a residual denoising autoencoder with generative text inversion where the denoiser is trained in an unsupervised manner using Stein's unbiased risk estimate to enable denoising from noisy observations alone. Extensive experiments show that DAEI achieves approximately 154\% relative improvement in BLEU over the existing generative inversion baseline, while also improving token-level F1 and ROUGE-L by 32--60\%. The promising inversion performance of DAEI challenges the prevailing assumption that simple Gaussian perturbation is sufficient to prevent sensitive information leakage from embedding representations.

</details>

*Artur Sokolovsky,Bhavik Merai,Moe Jafari,Muen Chen*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力网络日益面临天气敏感的失效机制，需要资产级、空间显式的风险建模以进行有效干预规划。现有模型在工业场景中难以兼顾操作性维护性与对数据可用性变化的适应性。

Method: 构建模块化、可扩展的资产级PoF建模架构，采用统一的地理空间机器学习管线，融合SRTM地形数据、MODIS NDVI植被指数、LIS VHRMC雷电气候学数据、OpenStreetMap邻近特征及电力运营记录等多元预测因子。

Result: 该框架计算高效、可操作性扩展，适用于公用事业规模部署，提供资产级风险分层，用于检查优先级排序、植被管理、资产加固和韧性规划。

Conclusion: 框架支持更早的干预和更具气候韧性的电网运行，为工业环境下维持可维护性的同时适配不断变化的灾害条件提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Electric power networks are increasingly exposed to weather-sensitive failure mechanisms that require asset-level, spatially explicit risk modelling for effective intervention planning. This study contributes a modular, robust, and explainable probability-of-failure (PoF) modelling framework for utility asset management. The central contribution is an asset-level architecture that can be scaled to new environmental data sources and additional PoF types without reworking the underlying pipeline. This is particularly relevant for industry settings, where risk models must remain operationally maintainable while adapting to changing data availability, asset-management priorities, and climate-driven hazard conditions. We demonstrate the framework for vegetation-related and lightning-related failure modes using a harmonised geospatial machine-learning pipeline. The implementation integrates multi-source predictors, including topography (SRTM), vegetation condition (MODIS Normalised Difference Vegetation Index - NDVI), lightning climatology (LIS VHRMC), OpenStreetMap-derived proximity features, and utility operational records. The resulting architecture is computationally efficient, operationally extensible, and suitable for utility-scale deployment. It provides actionable asset-level risk stratification for inspection prioritisation, vegetation management, asset hardening, and resilience planning, supporting earlier intervention and more climate-resilient network operations.

</details>


### [41] [Coordination on a Budget: Federated Active Learning with Few Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18634)
*Liam Mohr,Daphna Weinshall*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究跨孤岛联邦主动学习（FAL）在低标注预算场景下的查询选择问题，发现了一个"异质性反转"现象：低预算下同构（IID）数据反而比异构数据更需要协调以避免冗余查询，并据此提出基于联邦表示学习对齐客户端嵌入、由服务器进行全局协调选择的新框架，在低预算下超越需要更大预算的现有FAL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FAL同时面临数据隐私和标签稀缺的双重挑战，且缺乏全局数据视图使得协调查询选择更加困难。低预算场景下标注决策最为关键，但现有方法在此场景下性能不佳，因此需要深入理解低预算FAL的内在挑战并设计更有效的机制。

Method: 首先从理论和实证两个角度刻画了低预算FAL中的"异质性反转"现象：低预算下IID数据需要更强的协调以避免冗余查询，而异构数据天然促进多样性，但在高预算时趋势反转。基于此发现，提出利用联邦表示学习将客户端数据对齐到共享嵌入空间，使服务器能够在客户端（可选混淆的）嵌入上进行全局协调的主动选择，同时标注仍保留在各客户端本地。

Result: 所提出的框架在更具挑战性的低预算场景下运行，其性能超越了即使获得更大标注预算的现有FAL方法，验证了隐私约束下集中式协调的价值。

Conclusion: 该研究颠覆了标准FL中"异构性是主要挑战"的叙事，揭示了在FAL低预算设置下IID设置反而更具挑战性，并证明了通过联邦表示学习实现服务器端全局协调查询选择的有效性。

Abstract: Federated Active Learning (FAL) addresses the dual challenges of data privacy and label scarcity, where the absence of a global data view introduces additional hurdles for coordinated query selection. We study cross-silo FAL in the low-budget regime, where annotation decisions are most critical. We characterize, both theoretically and empirically, a heterogeneity reversal: in low-budget settings, homogeneous (IID) data requires stronger coordination to avoid redundant queries, whereas heterogeneous data naturally promotes diversity; this trend reverses at higher budgets. Thus, in contrast to the standard federated learning (FL) narrative where heterogeneity is a primary challenge, we show that IID settings are more challenging for query selection in FAL.
  Motivated by these findings, we propose a new FAL framework that utilizes federated representation learning to align client data in a shared embedding space. This enables the server to perform globally coordinated active selection over optionally obfuscated client embeddings, while annotation remains local to each client. Although our framework operates in the more challenging low-budget regime, it achieves performance that surpasses existing FAL methods even when they are given substantially larger annotation budgets, demonstrating the value of centralized coordination under privacy constraints.

</details>


### [42] [ProxyGuard: Direct Reliability Inference for Randomized Data Release Mechanisms with Shared Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18643)
*Dipesh Tharu Mahato,Pramod Dhungana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了ProxyGuard框架，用于在从多个代理数据集中选择或评估数据生成机制时，同时控制两种错误（选到无效发布物、宣布生成器可靠但实际不可靠），通过预设有限风险和密封目标集实现有限样本的机制可靠性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者常从多个发布、变换或种子的代理数据集中做选择，但搜索可能让无效发布物看起来足够好，而单个足够好的发布物又不能证明其生成器是可靠的。现有方法无法同时控制这两种错误。

Method: ProxyGuard提供两种模式：命名发布模式（named-release mode）校正多重比较并认证特定发布物；直接共享目标模式（direct shared-target mode）评估共同目标上的独立机制抽样，给出有利分数率的下界，并减去无效发布物可能贡献的有利分数上界。条件于目标，发布分数独立，无需独立目标批次或对发布级p值依赖性做假设。推导了均值惩罚的尖锐性，并给出带加性目标浓度的平滑分数证书。

Result: 在注册的三要求研究中，直接模式在可靠性0.95下将功效从5.6%提升到64.2%；命名模式在高信号证据下仍然更强。前瞻性审计覆盖了全流程Rice-TVAE（每次抽样都重训练）和非表格文本机制。

Conclusion: ProxyGuard以有限样本方式同时控制选择误差和可靠性误判，适用于广泛的代理数据机制评估场景，包括重训练管线和非表格生成模型。

Abstract: Researchers often choose a proxy dataset from many releases, transformations, or seeds. Search can make an invalid release appear adequate, while one adequate release does not establish that its generator is reliable. ProxyGuard controls both errors using prespecified bounded risks and a sealed target set. Named-release mode corrects for multiplicity and certifies specific releases. Direct shared-target mode evaluates independent mechanism draws on a common target, lower-bounds their favorable-score rate, and subtracts a bound on favorable scores contributed by invalid releases. Conditional on the target, release scores are independent, yielding a finite-sample mechanism-reliability guarantee without independent target batches or assumptions on release-level $p$-value dependence. We show that the mean-only penalty is sharp and derive a smooth-score certificate with additive target concentration. In a registered three-requirement study, direct mode raises power from 5.6\% to 64.2\% at reliability 0.95, while named mode remains stronger under high-signal evidence. Prospective audits span full-pipeline Rice--TVAE, which retrains on every draw, and a non-tabular text mechanism.

</details>
